        }
        Err(snapback4_lang::emit::lean::LeanError::Unsupported(u)) => {
            eprintln!("outside the Lean model's fragment: {u}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn check(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    if options.flags.contains_key("store") && options.flags.contains_key("release") {
        return releases::check_release(options);
    }

    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get("emit-lean") {
        return emit_lean(file);
    }
    let project = project_or_exit();
    let started = Instant::now();
    package_versions::warn(&project);
    upgrade_history::report(&project, options.json);
    let mut bound = match fix::checked(if options.flags.contains_key("fix") { fix::compile(&project) } else { project.compile() }) {
        Ok(bound) => bound,
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    if !crate::exact_device::check(&project.root, &bound.schema, options.json) { return 1; }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.3 — check judges adoption against the dev store's active generation and rows, read-only
    match adoption::diagnostics(&project, &bound.schema, options.flags.get("store"), options.json || options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
        Ok((problems, notes)) => { bound.diagnostics.extend(problems); bound.notes.extend(notes); },
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("cannot check store adoption: {error}"); return 2; }
    }
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
        report_diagnostics(&bound, options.json, true, options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
        if !options.json { print_prediction(&bound, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
        return 1;
    }
    bound.notes.extend(readiness::notes(&project, &bound, options.flags.get("fixture")));
    bound.notes.extend(snapback4_lang::bind::residency::change_notes(&bound, &residency::previous(&project)));
    check_surface::report(&project, &bound, options.json);
    if let Err(error) = project.write_generated(&bound) {
        eprintln!("cannot write generated types: {error}");
        return 2;
    }
    if let Err(error) = project.check_server() {
        if options.json { println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": [{ "code": if error.starts_with("E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN") { "E_SERVER_TOOLCHAIN" } else { "E_SERVER_TYPECHECK" }, "message": error, "guide": "actions" }] })); }
        else { eprintln!("{error}"); }
        return 1;
    }
    // A native job's module is built here too (LLP 3000.005): the schema's
    // Rust types written, cargo run, the module checked against the jobs.
    let native = match crate::native::build(&project, &bound) {
        Ok(built) => built,
        Err(problem) => {
            if options.json {
                println!("{}", json!({ "ok": false, "problems": [{ "code": problem.code, "message": problem.message, "rewrite": problem.rewrite, "guide": "native" }] }));
            } else {
                eprintln!("{problem}\n");
                eprintln!("check: 1 problem");
            }
            return 1;
        }
    };
    check_surface::remember(&project, &bound);
    if let Err(error) = residency::remember(&project, &bound) { eprintln!("cannot write residency facts: {error}"); return 2; }
    let acquisition = if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        match residency::at_check(&project, &bound, options) {
            Ok(report) => report,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
        }
//! Starter leftovers on the init-first path: `init --web` (or `--expo`) wrote
//! the todo starter, then the project replaced `snapback/schema.q`. The
//! starter's own files that still match the bytes init wrote, but no longer
//! fit the schema, are leftovers: `check` names them and `init --adapt`
//! removes or rewrites them. A file the project changed is never touched.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — leftovers are judged by the starter's original bytes, never by name alone.
use super::starter::{self, Body, Platform};
use super::{OwnSchema, own_schema};
use std::path::Path;

/// One unmodified starter file that no longer fits the project's schema.
pub(crate) struct Leftover {
    pub path: &'static str,
    pub why: String,
    /// The text `init --adapt` writes; `None` removes the file.
    pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

/// Starter operations its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
const STARTER_OPERATIONS: [&str; 4] = ["list", "add", "toggle", "remove"];

/// Whether a schema still has the starter's `todos` table and the operations
/// its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
pub(super) fn fits_todos(own: &OwnSchema) -> bool {
    own.tables.iter().any(|table| table == "todos")
        && STARTER_OPERATIONS.iter().all(|op| own.operations.iter().any(|name| name == op))
}

/// What the schema in `dir` leaves of the starter, and the advice that goes
/// with it (a sign-in the project must write by hand). No schema: nothing.
pub(crate) fn leftovers(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>) {
    let (found, advice, _) = plan(dir);
    (found, advice)
}

fn plan(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let Ok(Some(own)) = own_schema(dir) else { return (Vec::new(), Vec::new(), Vec::new()) };
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    let mut modified = Vec::new();
    let todos = fits_todos(&own);
    for (path, text) in starter::FIXTURES {
        let current = read(dir, path);
        if current.as_ref().is_some_and(|current| *current != super::without_design_references(text)) { modified.push(path.to_owned()); }
        if !todos && current.is_some_and(|current| current == super::without_design_references(text)) {
            let what = if path.ends_with("todos.jsonl") { "rows for the todo starter's `todos` table" } else { "cases for the todo starter's list/add/toggle/remove" };
            found.push(Leftover { path, why: format!("the unmodified {what}; your snapback/*.q has no todos table with those operations, so `snapback4 test` would refuse it"), rewrite: None });
        }
    }
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        if !screens(dir, platform, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut advice) {
            for path in [platform.paths().0, platform.paths().1] { if read(dir, path).is_some() { modified.push(path.to_owned()); } }
        }
    }
    if let Some(text) = read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts") {
        let wanted = starter::native_smoke(own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests));
        let recognized = [false, true].into_iter().any(|password| text == starter::native_smoke(password));
        if !recognized { modified.push("src/native-smoke.ts".to_owned()); }
        if text != wanted && recognized {
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why: "the unmodified native smoke helper uses the previous schema's sign-in".into(), rewrite: Some(wanted) });
        }
    }
    readme(dir, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut modified);
    app_json(dir, &mut found);
    expo_favicon(dir, &mut found);
    (found, advice, modified)
}

/// The starter README, while it is still one init wrote (for either screen,
/// at the ports it names), when it describes another screen than the schema
/// now gets: the Todos README beside a project's schema, or the reverse.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): adapt rewrote the screen but left a README about todos and guest sign-in.
fn readme(dir: &Path, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, modified: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let platform = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Web } else if dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Expo } else { return };
    let title = super::title(dir);
    let personas = own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty();
    let wanted_body = if todos { Body::Todos } else { Body::Home(None) };
    for path in ["README.md", "README.snapback.md"] {
        let Some(text) = read(dir, path) else { continue };
        let port = number_after(&text, "127.0.0.1:");
        let web_port = number_after(&text, "localhost:");
        let ports = [(port, web_port), (None, web_port), (port, None), (None, None)];
        // The offline paragraph names the schema's synced tables when init
        // wrote it; any of them (or 0.4.22's unconditional claim) still matches.
        let masked = starter::without_offline(&text);
        let explicit_online = read(dir, "src/client.ts").is_some_and(|client| client.contains("writes: \"online\""));
        let matched = ports.into_iter().find(|&(port, web_port)| {
            [(Body::Todos, false), (Body::Home(None), false), (Body::Home(None), true)].iter()
                .any(|(body, personas)| [false, true].iter().any(|password| [false, true].iter().any(|online| {
                    let readme = starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, body, *personas, &starter::Offline::Unknown, *password);
                    let readme = if *online { starter::online_readme(&readme) } else { readme };
                    masked == starter::without_offline(&super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&readme), port, web_port))
                })))
        });
        let Some((port, web_port)) = matched else { modified.push(path.to_owned()); continue };
        let wanted = super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, &wanted_body, personas, &own.offline, own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests))), port, web_port);
        let wanted = if explicit_online { starter::online_readme(&wanted) } else { wanted };
        if wanted == text { continue; }
        let about = if todos { "the todo starter's operations, which your snapback/*.q declares again" } else { "your snapback/*.q and the Home screen, named after this directory" };
        let only_proof = super::proof::without_readme(&wanted) == text;
        let only_offline = !text.starts_with("# Todos") && starter::without_offline(&wanted) == masked;
        // A schema that does not compile right now says nothing about what it syncs.
        if only_offline && !only_proof && own.offline == starter::Offline::Unknown { continue; }
        let why = if only_proof {
            "the unmodified starter README lacks frontend/day-two proof instructions; adapt adds them".to_owned()
        } else if only_offline {
            "the unmodified starter README says what works offline for another schema; adapt rewrites that paragraph from the tables your snapback/*.q syncs".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("the unmodified starter README describes {}; adapt rewrites it to describe {about}", if text.starts_with("# Todos") { "the todo starter (its todos table and guest sign-in)" } else { "the Home screen for another schema" })
        };
        found.push(Leftover { path, why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
    }
}

/// An Expo `app.json` that still carries the todo starter's slug, scheme and
/// bundle ids (written by 0.4.21 and earlier): adapt names them after the
/// directory and changes nothing else in the file.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (067): adapted Expo apps kept dev.snapback4.todos.
fn app_json(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    let expo = &config["expo"];
    let [(_, slug), (_, scheme), (_, bundle)] = starter::TODO_IDS;
    if expo["slug"] != slug || expo["scheme"] != scheme || expo["ios"]["bundleIdentifier"] != bundle || expo["android"]["package"] != bundle { return; }
    let (new_slug, new_scheme, new_bundle) = starter::app_ids(&super::title(dir));
    let quoted = |value: &str| format!("\"{value}\"");
    let rewrite = text.replace(&quoted(bundle), &quoted(&new_bundle)).replace(&quoted(slug), &quoted(&new_slug)).replace(&quoted(scheme), &quoted(&new_scheme));
    found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: format!("the todo starter's ids (slug {slug}, scheme {scheme}, bundle id {bundle}); adapt names them after this directory ({new_slug}, {new_scheme}, {new_bundle})"), rewrite: Some(rewrite) });
}

// Repair only the exact SVG asset and setting emitted by 0.4.26. Custom
// images/settings remain owned by the app, including other SVG paths.
fn expo_favicon(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    if read(dir, "assets/favicon.svg").as_deref() != Some(include_str!("starter/favicon.svg")) { return; }
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    if config.pointer("/expo/web/favicon").and_then(|value| value.as_str()) != Some("./assets/favicon.svg") { return; }
    let why = "the unmodified Expo favicon is SVG, which Expo's web export requires to be raster".to_owned();
    if let Some(existing) = found.iter_mut().find(|change| change.path == "app.json") {
        let mut config: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(existing.rewrite.as_ref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        existing.rewrite = Some(super::pretty(&config));
        existing.why.push_str("; Expo's web export also requires a raster favicon");
    } else {
        let mut config = config;
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: why.clone(), rewrite: Some(super::pretty(&config)) });
    }
    found.push(Leftover { path: "assets/favicon.svg", why, rewrite: None });
}

fn number_after(text: &str, prefix: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let at = text.find(prefix)? + prefix.len();
    let digits: String = text[at..].chars().take_while(char::is_ascii_digit).collect();
    digits.parse().ok()
}

/// The sign-in a schema admits, as the starter screen it needs: the guest
/// screen (`None`), the persona picker (`Some(personas)`), or no starter
/// screen fits (`Err(advice)`).
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum SignIn { Guest, Password, Personas(Vec<String>) }
impl SignIn {
    fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self { Self::Guest => "the guest sign-in".into(), Self::Password => "the password sign-in".into(), Self::Personas(names) => format!("a persona picker ({})", names.join(", ")) }
// Build first: npm run build (web) or npm run build:web (Expo web).
// Replace the application assertions when replacing the starter screen.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { startDev, startStatic, withBrowser, fillRN, statusText, waitForFresh, bounded, cleanup } from "snapback4/proof";

const dev = await startDev();
let web;
try {
  const identity = await (await fetch(`${dev.url}/auth/config`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) })).json();
  const personas = (await (await fetch(`${dev.url}/dev/status`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) })).json()).personas;
  web = await startStatic("dist");
  await withBrowser({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME }, async context => {
    const page = await context.newPage();
    await page.goto(`${web.url}/?api=${encodeURIComponent(dev.url)}&witness=web`, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
    if (identity.guests) {
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue as a guest", exact: true }).click();
    } else if (identity.password) {
      await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Email", { exact: true }), `proof-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
      await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Password", { exact: true }), "Proof-password-928!");
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign up", exact: true }).click();
    } else {
      assert.ok(personas.length, "declare identity or personas before proving the starter");
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: personas[0], exact: true }).click();
    }
    // TEMPLATE_READY
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^(Sign out|Switch persona)$/, exact: true }).waitFor();
    const task = page.getByLabel("Task", { exact: true });
    if (await task.count()) {
      const title = `Proof ${randomUUID()}`;
      await fillRN(task, title);
      // Arm the app's SDK receipt outside the form, which may unmount.
      const button = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add", exact: true });
      await button.evaluate((element, title) => window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm("add", { title }, element), title);
      await button.click();
      const receipt = await bounded(page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.formResult()), 30_000, "clicked write receipt");
      assert.equal(receipt.state, "sent", JSON.stringify(receipt));
      await waitForFresh(statusText(page, new RegExp(title)), null);
    }
  });
  console.log("frontend:proof passed");
} finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }
// Runtime launcher: copied apps read their current owner's range on every run.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { dirname, join } from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { resolveBinary } from "snapback4/bin-path";
import { portRange, stopChild } from "snapback4/proof";
const require = createRequire(new URL("../package.json", import.meta.url));
const mode = process.argv[2];
const range = portRange();
const low = range === "0" ? null : Number(range.split("-")[0]);
const high = range === "0" ? null : Number(range.split("-")[1]);
if (low !== null && high === low && !["backend", "native-smoke"].includes(mode)) throw Error("the starter needs two runtime ports");
const port = ["backend", "native-smoke"].includes(mode) ? low === null ? BACKEND : low : low === null ? FRONTEND : low + 1;
const command = ["backend", "native-smoke"].includes(mode) ? resolveBinary() : process.execPath;
const args = mode === "native-smoke" ? ["witness", "ios", "--build", ".", "--smoke", ...(low === null ? [] : ["--port", String(port)])]
  : mode === "backend" ? ["dev", "--port", String(port)]
  : mode.startsWith("vite") ? [join(dirname(require.resolve("vite/package.json")), "bin/vite.js"), ...(mode === "vite-preview" ? ["preview"] : []), "--port", String(port), "--strictPort"]
  : [join(dirname(require.resolve("expo/package.json")), "bin/cli"), "start", ...(mode === "expo-web" ? ["--web"] : []), "--port", String(port)];
const env = { ...process.env };
if (low !== null) {
  env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL = `http://127.0.0.1:${low}`;
  env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL = env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL;
}
const child = spawn(command, args, { stdio: "inherit", env });
process.once("exit", () => child.kill("SIGKILL"));
for (const signal of ["SIGTERM", "SIGINT"]) process.once(signal, () => {
  stopChild(child).then(() => process.exit(0), error => { console.error(error); process.exit(1); });
});
child.on("error", error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });
child.once("close", code => { process.exitCode = code ?? 1; });

+        let plan = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run"]));
+        assert!(plan.contains("README.md") && plan.contains("lacks frontend/day-two proof instructions"), "{plan}");
+        ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--apply"])); assert_eq!(dir.text("README.md"), current);
+        fs::write(dir.0.join("README.md"), &historical).unwrap();
+        fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  body: text <=80\n  public 'shared'\n").unwrap();
+        let plan = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run"]));
+        assert!(plan.contains("README.md") && !plan.contains("modified files"), "{plan}");
+        ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--apply"]));
+        let adapted = dir.text("README.md"); assert!(adapted.contains("Home") && adapted.contains("frontend:proof"));
+        fs::write(dir.0.join("README.md"), format!("{adapted}\nMy notes.\n")).unwrap();
+        ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--apply"])); assert!(dir.text("README.md").ends_with("My notes.\n"));
+    }
+}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#9 — one range localizes every starter launcher before any file is written.
use super::super::Options;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

pub(super) fn range(options: &Options) -> Result<Option<(u16, u16)>, String> {
    // Environment belongs to the process launch, never to generated files.
    // Validate malformed assigned ranges at run time through snapback4/proof.
    let text = options.flags.get("port-range").cloned();
    let Some(text) = text else { return Ok(None) };
    let (low, high) = text.split_once('-').ok_or("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)")?;
    let (low, high) = (low.parse::<u16>(), high.parse::<u16>());
    match (low, high) {
        (Ok(low), Ok(high)) if low > 0 && low <= high => Ok(Some((low, high))),
        _ => Err("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)".into()),
    }
}

pub(super) fn scripts(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, backend: u16, frontend: u16, range: Option<(u16, u16)>, web: bool) {
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name != "package.json" { continue; }
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!(format!("snapback4 dev --port {backend}"));
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!(format!("vite --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!(format!("vite preview --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!(format!("expo start --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!(format!("expo start --web --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness ios --build . --smoke --port {backend}"));
        }
        package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness web dist{}", range.map(|(low, high)| format!(" --port-range {low}-{high}")).unwrap_or_default()));
        *text = super::pretty(&package);

/// The page and app name: the directory's, as words ("bake-sale" → "Bake Sale").
/// A starter file with init's ports: the backend's (`--port`) and the Vite
/// page's (`--web-port`). A README's `npx snapback4 dev` names the port too.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): the README said plain `snapback4 dev` beside a client on another port.
pub(super) fn localize(name: &str, text: &str, port: Option<u16>, web_port: Option<u16>) -> String {
    let mut text = text.to_owned();
    if let Some(port) = port {
        text = text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}"));
        if name.starts_with("README") { text = text.replace("npx snapback4 dev  ", &format!("npx snapback4 dev --port {port}  ")); }
    }
    if let Some(web_port) = web_port {
        text = text.replace("port: 5173", &format!("port: {web_port}")).replace("port: 4173", &format!("port: {web_port}")).replace("localhost:5173", &format!("localhost:{web_port}"));
    }
    text
}

pub(super) fn title(dir: &Path) -> String {
    let path = std::fs::canonicalize(dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| std::path::absolute(dir).unwrap_or_else(|_| dir.to_path_buf()));
    let name = path.file_name().map(|name| name.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    let words: Vec<String> = name.split(|c: char| c == '-' || c == '_' || c == '.' || c.is_whitespace()).filter(|word| !word.is_empty())
        .map(|word| { let mut chars = word.chars(); chars.next().map(|first| first.to_uppercase().chain(chars).collect()).unwrap_or_default() }).collect();
    if words.is_empty() { "My app".into() } else { words.join(" ") }
}

/// Vite's config is `vite.config.mts`, so the reference tsconfig names that.
fn esm_vite_config(tsconfig: &str) -> String { tsconfig.replace("\"vite.config.ts\"", "\"vite.config.mts\"").replace("\"types\": []", "\"types\": [\"node\"]") }

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#10 — packed starters must pass their real web toolchains
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:net";
import { Worker } from "node:worker_threads";
import { loadavg } from "node:os";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve, join } from "node:path";

// The live keeper alone signals its own group. Closing its private pipe also
// cleans up if this runner dies; no cached PID ever authorizes a signal.
export async function runToolchainCommand(command, args, { cwd, env, timeout }) {
  const owner = fileURLToPath(new URL("./test-command-owner.mjs", import.meta.url));
  const keeper = spawn(process.execPath, [owner, "100", command, ...args], { cwd, env, detached: true, stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit", "ipc"] });
  let released = false, completed = false, failure, status;
  const release = mode => { if (!released) { released = true; keeper.stdin.end(mode); } };
  const fail = error => { failure ??= error; release("TERM"); };
  const timer = setTimeout(() => fail(Error(`${command}: toolchain command timed out`)), timeout);
  keeper.stdin.on("error", error => { failure ??= error; });
  keeper.on("message", message => {
    if (message.error) fail(Error(message.error));
    if (message.exited) { completed = true; status = message.status; release("KILL"); }
  });
  await new Promise(done => {
    keeper.once("error", error => { failure ??= error; });
    keeper.once("close", done);
  });
  clearTimeout(timer);
  if (!released || !completed) failure ??= Error(`${command}: command owner exited without a result`);
  // This read-only group-existence probe needs no /proc mount in the clean
  // userspace. Refuse on lingering members or ambiguous/permission errors.
  // Two empty observations also protect against a transient inspection result.
  const deadline = performance.now() + 5_000;
  let empty = 0;
  if (keeper.pid) while (empty < 2) {
    try { process.kill(-keeper.pid, 0); empty = 0; }
    catch (error) { if (error.code === "ESRCH") empty++; else { failure ??= error; break; } }
    if (empty === 2) break;
    if (performance.now() >= deadline) { failure ??= Error(`${command}: toolchain process group did not drain`); break; }
    await sleep(50);
  }
  if (failure) throw failure;
  if (status !== 0) throw Error(`${command}: toolchain command exited ${status}`);
}

// Every supported frontend/template pair; schema-only init has no frontend proof.
export const starterCases = [
  { id: "web", kind: "web", flags: ["--web"] },
  { id: "web-online", kind: "web", flags: ["--web", "--online"] },
  { id: "expo", kind: "expo", flags: ["--expo"] },
  { id: "web-chat", kind: "web", template: "chat", flags: ["--web", "--template", "chat"] },
  { id: "expo-chat", kind: "expo", template: "chat", flags: ["--expo", "--template", "chat"] },
  { id: "expo-social", kind: "expo", template: "social", flags: ["--template", "social"] },
];
async function boundedStop(work, label) {
  let timer;
  try { return await Promise.race([work, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(label + " timed out")), 10_000); })]); }
  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
export async function proveFrontendTwice(run, cwd, starter) {
  const results = [];
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt++) {
    const occupied = createServer();
    const low = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { occupied.once("error", reject); occupied.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => resolve(occupied.address().port)); });
    const high = Math.min(65535, low + 20);
    const workers = Array.from({ length: 2 }, () => new Worker(`setInterval(() => { const until = performance.now() + 25; while (performance.now() < until) {} }, 50);`, { eval: true }));
    const started = Date.now(), load = loadavg();
    try {
      await run("npm", ["run", "frontend:proof"], cwd, { PORT_LO: String(low), PORT_HI: String(high) });
      results.push({ starter: starter.id, kind: starter.kind, attempt, status: "passed", elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, range: [low, high], occupied: low, cpuLoadWorkers: workers.length, loadavg: load, logs: join(cwd, "logs") });
    } finally {
      const stopped = await Promise.allSettled([...workers.map(worker => boundedStop(worker.terminate(), "CPU worker stop")), boundedStop(new Promise(resolve => occupied.close(resolve)), "occupied listener close")]);
      const failures = stopped.filter(result => result.status === "rejected").map(result => result.reason);
      if (failures.length) throw new AggregateError(failures, "loaded-host proof cleanup failed");
    }
  }
  return results;
}

      await run("npm", ["run", kind === "expo" ? "build:web" : "build"], cwd);
      assert.ok(existsSync(join(cwd, "dist/index.html")), `${kind} produced a web page`);
    };
    await build();
    const frontendProofs = browserProofs ? await proveFrontendTwice(run, cwd, starter) : [];
    if (template || id === "web-online") {
#!/usr/bin/env node
// `npx snapback4 <verb>`: the Rust binary, found in the platform package
// installed beside this one, or named by SNAPBACK4_BIN, or built in this
// repository. The JavaScript here only finds and runs it, and the two live and
// die together: SIGINT, SIGTERM and SIGHUP to this wrapper are forwarded, and
// SIGKILL, which cannot be forwarded, still stops the binary: it is told this
// wrapper's PID (SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID) and exits when its parent is gone
// (Linux: the kernel's parent-death signal; elsewhere within 250 ms). So a
// recorded PID of either stops the server; `GET /dev/status` reports both
// (`pid` is the binary, `wrapperPid` this wrapper), and so does the stderr line
// `SNAPBACK4_READY … url=… pid=… wrapperPid=…`.
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { resolveBinary } from "../dist/resolve-binary.js";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { constants } from "node:os";

if (process.argv[2] === "web-shell") {
  try { await (await import("./web-shell.js")).runWebShell(process.argv.slice(3)); }
  catch (error) { console.error(`E_INPUT at web-shell: ${error.message}\n  rewrite: export a static app, then run snapback4 web-shell dist --base /`); process.exit(2); }
  process.exit(0);
}

let binary;
try { binary = resolveBinary(); }
catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exit(2); }
// Internal export helpers share the wrapper's kernel lease through fd 3.
const exportLease = process.argv[2] === "witness" && ["export-lease", "export-publish"].includes(process.argv[3]);
const child = spawn(binary, process.argv.slice(2), { stdio: exportLease ? ["inherit", "inherit", "inherit", 3] : "inherit", env: {
  ...process.env, SNAPBACK4_ACQUIRE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-acquire.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./upgrade.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_WEB_HELPER: fileURLToPath(new URL("./witness-web.js", import.meta.url)),
  SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid),
} });
const handlers = new Map(["SIGINT", "SIGTERM", "SIGHUP"].map(signal => [signal, () => { try { child.kill(signal); } catch { /* gone */ } }]));
for (const [signal, handler] of handlers) process.on(signal, handler);
// `npx snapback4 dev` puts npm and `sh -c` above this wrapper. A SIGTERM to the
// npx PID kills npm and the shell but never reaches us, so the server would keep
// serving as an orphan. Our parent's death is the stop signal. (`process.ppid` is
// read once at startup, so probe the original parent instead.)
const parent = process.ppid;
const parentAlive = () => { try { process.kill(parent, 0); return true; } catch (error) { return error.code === "EPERM"; } };
const orphanWatch = parent > 1 ? setInterval(() => {
  if (!parentAlive()) { try { child.kill("SIGTERM"); } catch { /* gone */ } }
}, 250) : null;
orphanWatch?.unref();
child.on("exit", (code, signal) => {
  if (orphanWatch) clearInterval(orphanWatch);
  for (const [name, handler] of handlers) process.off(name, handler);
  if (signal) {
    // Restore the default disposition before re-raising. Some platforms do not
    // terminate on this call; never let their empty event loop report success.
    const status = 128 + (constants.signals[signal] ?? { SIGHUP: 1, SIGINT: 2, SIGTERM: 15 }[signal] ?? 1);
    setTimeout(() => process.exit(status), 100);
    try { process.kill(process.pid, signal); } catch { process.exit(status); }
    return;
  }
  process.exit(code ?? 1);
});
